      "id": "Caballo Marino Chilote adalah makhluk air dalam mitologi Chilote yang agak mirip hippocampus [caballo-marino-chilote-c01].       "en": "The Caballo Marino Chilote is an aquatic creature of Chilote mythology resembling the hippocampus [caballo-marino-chilote-c01].     {
      "id": "caballo-marino-chilote-c01",
      "source_id": "caballo-marino-chilote-s1",
      "quote": "The Caballo Marino Chilote (chilote sea horse) is an aquatic creature of the Chilote mythology of Chile, that bears some resemblance to the hippocampus.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Caballo Marino Chilote (\"kuda laut Chilote\") adalah makhluk air dalam mitologi Chilote di Chile yang agak mirip hippocampus.",
        "en": "The Caballo Marino Chilote (\"Chilote sea horse\") is an aquatic creature of Chilote mythology in Chile that resembles the hippocampus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballo-marino-chilote-c02",
      "source_id": "caballo-marino-chilote-s1",
      "quote": "Legend says that the \"Caballo marino chilote\" is an invisible creature, which could only be seen by those with magical powers.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, makhluk ini tak kasatmata dan hanya dapat dilihat oleh orang berkekuatan gaib.",
        "en": "Legend says it is invisible and can only be seen by those with magical powers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballo-marino-chilote-c03",
      "source_id": "caballo-marino-chilote-s1",
      "quote": "The creature would look like a normal horse, but would have the longer snout, golden mane, four paws in the form of fins, and a long tail, similar to the tail of a fish.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wujudnya seperti kuda biasa, tetapi bermoncong lebih panjang, bersurai emas, berkaki empat berbentuk sirip, dan berekor panjang seperti ekor ikan.",
        "en": "It looks like a normal horse but with a longer snout, golden mane, four fin-like paws and a long fish-like tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballo-marino-chilote-c04",
      "source_id": "caballo-marino-chilote-s1",
      "quote": "They can exist in various sizes, from dwarfs to giants.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ukurannya beragam, dari kerdil hingga raksasa.",
        "en": "They exist in various sizes, from dwarfs to giants."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballo-marino-chilote-c05",
      "source_id": "caballo-marino-chilote-s1",
      "quote": "The Brujo Chilote (a type of sorcerer or warlock) would use a \"Caballo marino chilote\" as transportation to get to the Caleuche ghost ship.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Brujo Chilote (sejenis penyihir) memakainya sebagai tunggangan untuk mencapai kapal hantu Caleuche.",
        "en": "The Brujo Chilote (a sorcerer) rides it to reach the Caleuche ghost ship."
      }
    }

      "en": "The Caboclo de Agua is said to live in a golden grotto at the bottom of the São Francisco River, frightening fishermen and boatmen [caboclo-de-agua-c01].   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c01",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "El Caboclo de Agua es un ser mitológico del nordeste de Brasil que supuestamente habita en el fondo de las aguas del Río São Francisco en una gruta de oro y se dedica a asustar a pescadores y navegantes.",
      "locator": "Introducción",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Caboclo de Agua adalah makhluk mitologi dari timur laut Brasil yang konon tinggal di dasar Sungai São Francisco dalam gua emas dan menakut-nakuti nelayan serta pelaut.",
        "en": "The Caboclo de Agua is a mythological being of northeastern Brazil said to live at the bottom of the São Francisco River in a golden grotto, frightening fishermen and boatmen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c02",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "Los nativos lo describen como un ser gigante y musculoso, con piel de color bronce y un único ojo gigante en la cabeza.",
      "locator": "Forma",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penduduk setempat menggambarkannya sebagai makhluk raksasa berotot, berkulit perunggu, dengan satu mata besar di kepala.",
        "en": "Locals describe it as a giant, muscular being with bronze skin and a single huge eye."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c03",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "Tiene un corazón muy duro impenetrable a las balas.",
      "locator": "Forma",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jantungnya sangat keras dan tak tembus peluru.",
        "en": "Its heart is very hard and impenetrable to bullets."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c04",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "También puede aparecer bajo la apariencia de otros animales, como caballos.",
      "locator": "Forma",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dapat muncul dalam wujud hewan lain, seperti kuda.",
        "en": "It can also appear as other animals, such as horses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c05",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "Cuando no le cae bien un pescador, ahuyenta a la pesca de las redes. Cuando le cae bien algún hombre, logra que se le llenen las redes.",
      "locator": "Leyenda",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jika tidak menyukai seorang nelayan, ia mengusir ikan dari jaringnya; jika menyukainya, ia membuat jaringnya penuh.",
        "en": "If it dislikes a fisherman it drives fish from his nets; if it likes him, it fills them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c06",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "A menudo persigue a las embarcaciones para hacerlas volcar.",
      "locator": "Leyenda",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia sering mengejar perahu untuk membalikkannya.",
        "en": "It often chases boats to capsize them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c07",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "Para ahuyentarlo, los pescadores del río construyen en la proa de sus barcos, figuras monstruosas llamadas \"carrancas\".",
      "locator": "Leyenda",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengusirnya, nelayan memasang figur seram bernama \"carrancas\" di haluan perahu.",
        "en": "To ward it off, river fishermen put monstrous figures called \"carrancas\" on their bows."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caboclo-de-agua-c08",
      "source_id": "caboclo-de-agua-s1",
      "quote": "También pintan una estrella en el fondo de la barca pues se cree que espanta a los seres sobrenaturales.",
      "locator": "Leyenda",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka juga melukis bintang di dasar perahu karena dipercaya menakuti makhluk gaib.",
        "en": "They also paint a star on the bottom of the boat, believed to scare supernatural beings."
      }
    }

    "en": "Chasca, \"the virgin of the water\", is a maiden spirit of Salvadoran folklore who appears in a white canoe on full-moon nights [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c01, chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c06].",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Chasca, yang dijuluki \"perawan air\", adalah tokoh cerita rakyat El Salvador [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c01]. Ayahnya, Pachacutec, menjodohkannya dengan pangeran zutuhil [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c02], padahal ia mencintai nelayan muda Acayatl [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c03]. Setelah Acayatl dibunuh oleh utusan Pachacutec [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c04], Chasca menenggelamkan diri dengan batu terikat di pinggang [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c05].",
      "en": "Chasca, called \"the virgin of the water\", is a character of Salvadoran folklore [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c01]. Her father Pachacutec arranged her marriage to a zutuhil prince [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c02], but she loved the young fisher Acayatl [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c03]. After Acayatl was killed by an assassin sent by Pachacutec [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c04], Chasca drowned herself with a rock tied to her waist [chasca-salvadoran-folklore-c05].",

      "id": "Dorlis adalah makhluk mitologi dari beberapa negara Afrika dan Hindia Barat Prancis [dorlis-c01].       "en": "The Dorlis is a mythological creature of some African countries and the French West Indies [dorlis-c01].       "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "The Dorlis (or husband of the night) is a mythological creature that appears in the culture of certain African countries and in the French West Indies (Martinique and Guadeloupe).",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dorlis (\"suami malam\") adalah makhluk mitologi dalam budaya beberapa negara Afrika dan Hindia Barat Prancis (Martinique dan Guadeloupe).",
        "en": "The Dorlis (\"husband of the night\") is a mythological creature in the culture of some African countries and the French West Indies (Martinique and Guadeloupe)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c02",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "It is an impure and invisible spirit that sexually harasses women (and sometimes men) at night, physically overpowering them and disturbing their rest.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia roh najis tak kasatmata yang melecehkan perempuan (kadang laki-laki) secara seksual pada malam hari dan mengganggu tidur mereka.",
        "en": "It is an impure, invisible spirit that sexually harasses women (and sometimes men) at night, disturbing their rest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c03",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "Its equivalent in European legends would be the incubus and succubus",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Padanannya dalam legenda Eropa adalah incubus dan succubus.",
        "en": "Its European equivalents are the incubus and succubus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c04",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "According to Martiniquan beliefs, the Dorlis can enter houses through cracks or keyholes using magical methods.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan Martinique, Dorlis dapat masuk rumah lewat celah atau lubang kunci dengan cara gaib.",
        "en": "In Martiniquan belief, the Dorlis can enter houses through cracks or keyholes by magic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c05",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "methods exist to avoid its attack, such as sleeping in black underwear or with nightclothes worn inside out.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Serangannya dapat dihindari dengan tidur memakai pakaian dalam hitam atau baju tidur terbalik.",
        "en": "Its attack can be avoided by sleeping in black underwear or with nightclothes inside out."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c06",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "Another way to mislead the Dorlis is to place a container of salt near the bed, as the Dorlis would have to count every grain in it before it could act",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cara lain adalah menaruh wadah garam di dekat tempat tidur karena Dorlis harus menghitung setiap butirnya sebelum bertindak.",
        "en": "Another way is to put a container of salt by the bed, since the Dorlis must count every grain before acting."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c07",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "The Dorlis is sometimes used as an excuse for illicit relationships, but it can also be the manifestation of a psychological problem.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dorlis kadang dijadikan dalih hubungan terlarang, tetapi juga bisa menjadi wujud masalah psikologis.",
        "en": "The Dorlis is sometimes an excuse for illicit relationships, but can also manifest a psychological problem."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dorlis-c08",
      "source_id": "dorlis-s1",
      "quote": "The figure of the Dorlis is used by writers to reveal the fears and contradictions of French Antillean society.",

      "statement": {

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cressie-c01",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "In Canadian folklore, Cressie is the nickname given to an eel-like lake monster said to reside in Crescent Lake, Robert's Arm, Newfoundland and Labrador, Canada.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Kanada, Cressie adalah julukan monster danau mirip belut yang konon hidup di Crescent Lake, Robert's Arm, Newfoundland and Labrador.",
        "en": "In Canadian folklore, Cressie is an eel-like lake monster said to live in Crescent Lake, Robert's Arm, Newfoundland and Labrador."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c02",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "The name is a portmanteau of Crescent Lake and Nessie, the nickname given to the Loch Ness Monster.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya gabungan dari Crescent Lake dan Nessie, julukan Loch Ness Monster.",
        "en": "The name blends Crescent Lake and Nessie, the Loch Ness Monster's nickname."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c03",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "The monster has been described as resembling a large dark brown eel around 15 feet in length with a long, sleek body",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini digambarkan mirip belut besar cokelat tua sepanjang sekitar 15 kaki dengan tubuh panjang dan licin.",
        "en": "It has been described as a large dark brown eel about 15 feet long with a long, sleek body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c04",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "Claims of Cressie being sighted began in the 1950s, and continue to the present day.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan penampakan Cressie dimulai pada tahun 1950-an dan berlanjut hingga kini.",
        "en": "Sightings of Cressie began in the 1950s and continue today."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c05",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "some have linked the legends to earlier Indigenous legends of the woodum haoot (\"pond devil\") or haoot tuwedyee (\"swimming demon\")",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian orang mengaitkannya dengan legenda pribumi woodum haoot (\"iblis kolam\") atau haoot tuwedyee (\"iblis berenang\").",
        "en": "Some link it to earlier Indigenous legends of the woodum haoot (\"pond devil\") or haoot tuwedyee (\"swimming demon\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c06",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "others caution that this attribution has seemingly been copied from source to source without any verification",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Yang lain mengingatkan bahwa kaitan itu tampaknya disalin dari sumber ke sumber tanpa verifikasi.",
        "en": "Others caution that this link seems copied from source to source without verification."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c07",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "There have been no photographs of Cressie, and all information relies on local oral history.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak ada foto Cressie; semua informasi bergantung pada sejarah lisan setempat.",
        "en": "There are no photographs of Cressie; all information relies on local oral history."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cressie-c08",
      "source_id": "cressie-s1",
      "quote": "On July 9, 1991, Fred Parsons and his wife reported seeing a large snakelike creature swimming in Crescent Lake.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 9 Juli 1991, Fred Parsons dan istrinya melaporkan melihat makhluk besar mirip ular berenang di Crescent Lake.",
        "en": "On July 9, 1991, Fred Parsons and his wife reported a large snakelike creature swimming in Crescent Lake."
      }
    }

    "id": "Crybaby Bridge adalah sebutan bagi banyak jembatan di Amerika Serikat yang dikaitkan dengan cerita hantu tentang suara tangis bayi [crybaby-bridge-c01].",
    "en": "Crybaby Bridge is the name of many bridges in the United States tied to ghost stories of a baby crying [crybaby-bridge-c01].",
    "claim_ids": [

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Crybaby Bridge adalah sebutan bagi banyak jembatan di Amerika Serikat yang dikaitkan dengan cerita hantu tentang tangis bayi [crybaby-bridge-c01]. Kisahnya biasanya berlatar tragedi seperti pembunuhan bayi atau kecelakaan [crybaby-bridge-c02], dan setiap daerah menyesuaikannya dengan sejarah setempat [crybaby-bridge-c03].",
      "en": "Crybaby Bridge names many bridges in the United States tied to ghost stories of a crying baby [crybaby-bridge-c01]. The tales usually rest on tragedies such as infanticide or accidents [crybaby-bridge-c02], and each region adapts them to local history [crybaby-bridge-c03].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "crybaby-bridge-c01",
        "crybaby-bridge-c02",
        "crybaby-bridge-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Contohnya jembatan di Sleepy Hollow Road, Kentucky [crybaby-bridge-c04], dan jembatan dekat De Kalb, Texas, tempat seorang bayi konon tenggelam setelah mobil ibunya terjun ke sungai [crybaby-bridge-c05].",
      "en": "Examples include a bridge on Sleepy Hollow Road, Kentucky [crybaby-bridge-c04], and one near De Kalb, Texas, where a baby supposedly drowned after its mother's car plunged into the creek [crybaby-bridge-c05].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "crybaby-bridge-c04",
        "crybaby-bridge-c05"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "crybaby-bridge-s1",
      "quote": "Crybaby Bridge refers to numerous bridges across the United States, associated with urban legends and ghost stories involving the sounds of a baby crying.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Crybaby Bridge adalah sebutan bagi banyak jembatan di Amerika Serikat yang dikaitkan dengan legenda urban dan cerita hantu tentang suara tangis bayi.",
        "en": "Crybaby Bridge refers to many bridges across the United States tied to urban legends and ghost stories of a baby crying."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crybaby-bridge-c02",
      "source_id": "crybaby-bridge-s1",
      "quote": "These tales typically involve tragic backstories of infanticide, accidents, or other sorrowful events that purportedly occurred at or near the bridges",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah-kisah ini biasanya berlatar tragedi pembunuhan bayi, kecelakaan, atau peristiwa sedih lain di dekat jembatan.",
        "en": "These tales usually involve tragic backstories of infanticide, accidents or other sorrowful events near the bridges."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crybaby-bridge-c03",
      "source_id": "crybaby-bridge-s1",
      "quote": "The phenomenon is not limited to a specific location, but represents a type of folklore that has become embedded in the cultural fabric of various regions, each adapting the legend to fit local histories or landscapes.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Fenomena ini tidak terbatas pada satu tempat; ini jenis cerita rakyat yang disesuaikan tiap daerah dengan sejarah dan bentang alamnya.",
        "en": "It is not tied to one place but is a type of folklore each region adapts to its own history and landscape."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "crybaby-bridge-c04",
      "source_id": "crybaby-bridge-s1",
      "quote": "A bridge on Sleepy Hollow Road near the border between Jefferson and Oldham counties in Kentucky was known as Crybaby Bridge.",
      "locator": "Kentucky",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah jembatan di Sleepy Hollow Road di perbatasan Jefferson dan Oldham, Kentucky, dikenal sebagai Crybaby Bridge.",
        "en": "A bridge on Sleepy Hollow Road between Jefferson and Oldham counties, Kentucky, was known as Crybaby Bridge."
      }
    },

      "source_id": "crybaby-bridge-s1",
      "quote": "Legend says that a mother driving a car plunged into the creek, and the baby drowned in the near-freezing waters.",

      "en": "The Flathead Lake Monster is said to live in Flathead Lake, Montana [flathead-lake-monster-c01].       "en": "The Kutenai legend of an antlered monster under the ice."

  ],
  "images": [],
  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "url": "https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flathead_Lake_Monster",

      "author": null,

      "language": "en",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c01",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The Flathead Lake Monster is a lake monster of Indigenous legend and Montana folklore that is purported to dwell in Flathead Lake in northwestern Montana, United States.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Flathead Lake Monster adalah monster danau dari legenda pribumi dan cerita rakyat Montana yang konon hidup di Flathead Lake, Montana barat laut, Amerika Serikat.",
        "en": "The Flathead Lake Monster is a lake monster of Indigenous legend and Montana folklore said to live in Flathead Lake, northwestern Montana, United States."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c02",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The story of a monster in Flathead Lake originates in a Kutenai traditional legend.",
      "locator": "Native American legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah monster di Flathead Lake berasal dari legenda tradisional Kutenai.",
        "en": "The story originates in a Kutenai traditional legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c03",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The frozen surface of the lake split open around them, and the head of a monster appeared through the ice, shaking its giant antlers.",
      "locator": "Native American legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda itu, permukaan danau yang beku terbelah dan kepala monster bertanduk rusa raksasa muncul dari balik es.",
        "en": "In the legend, the frozen lake split open and a monster's head with giant antlers rose through the ice."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c04",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "but half of the tribe drowned in the lake, which is said to be the reason why there are so few Kutenai people.",
      "locator": "Native American legend",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Separuh suku tenggelam di danau, yang konon menjadi alasan jumlah orang Kutenai sedikit.",
        "en": "Half the tribe drowned, said to be why there are so few Kutenai people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c05",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "It was supposedly first reported in 1889 by Captain James C. Kerr of the lake steamboat U.S. Grant.",
      "locator": "Local stories",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini konon pertama kali dilaporkan pada tahun 1889 oleh Kapten James C. Kerr dari kapal uap U.S. Grant.",
        "en": "It was supposedly first reported in 1889 by Captain James C. Kerr of the steamboat U.S. Grant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c06",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "Fugleberg notes that local residents have named the purported lake monster \"Flessie\", inspired by Loch Ness' \"Nessie\".",
      "locator": "Local stories",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Fugleberg, warga setempat menamainya \"Flessie\", terilhami \"Nessie\" dari Loch Ness.",
        "en": "According to Fugleberg, locals named it \"Flessie\", inspired by Loch Ness's \"Nessie\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "flathead-lake-monster-c07",
      "source_id": "flathead-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The monster is usually described as a large, round, eel-shaped creature with a wavy body like a snake about twenty to forty feet (6.1 to 12.2 m) long, having brownish to bluish-black skin and grayish-black eyes.",
      "locator": "Local stories",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia biasanya digambarkan seperti belut besar bertubuh bergelombang seperti ular, panjang sekitar dua puluh hingga empat puluh kaki, berkulit cokelat hingga hitam kebiruan.",
        "en": "It is usually described as a large eel-shaped creature with a wavy, snake-like body about twenty to forty feet long and brownish to bluish-black skin."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

}

      "source_id": "flying-head-s1",
      "quote": "Flying Heads are described as being ravenous spirits that are cursed with an insatiable hunger.",

    "en": "The Fresno nightcrawler is a modern cryptid from Fresno, California, described as white, long-legged and like \"walking pairs of pants\" [fresno-nightcrawler-c01, fresno-nightcrawler-c02, fresno-nightcrawler-c03].",

      "id": "Fresno nightcrawler adalah kriptid yang konon berasal dari Fresno, California [fresno-nightcrawler-c01]. Ia digambarkan putih, berkaki panjang, dan biasanya berpasangan [fresno-nightcrawler-c02], seperti \"celana panjang yang berjalan\" [fresno-nightcrawler-c03]. Makhluk ini populer lewat rekaman CCTV tahun 2007 yang ditayangkan Univision [fresno-nightcrawler-c05].",
      "en": "This cryptid is claimed to come from Fresno, California [fresno-nightcrawler-c01]. It is described as white, long-legged and seen in pairs [fresno-nightcrawler-c02], like \"walking pairs of pants\" [fresno-nightcrawler-c03]. It became popular through 2007 CCTV footage aired on Univision [fresno-nightcrawler-c05].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "fresno-nightcrawler-c01",
        "fresno-nightcrawler-c02",
        "fresno-nightcrawler-c03",
        "fresno-nightcrawler-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Brian Dunning menyebutnya legenda urban [fresno-nightcrawler-c04], dan upaya mengaitkannya dengan legenda pribumi tidak dikenali oleh suku setempat [fresno-nightcrawler-c06]. Kini ia menjadi ikon Fresno dengan penggemar di seluruh dunia [fresno-nightcrawler-c07].",
      "en": "Brian Dunning calls it an urban legend [fresno-nightcrawler-c04], and attempts to link it to Indigenous legends were not recognized by local tribes [fresno-nightcrawler-c06]. It is now an icon of Fresno with fans worldwide [fresno-nightcrawler-c07].",

      "quote": "The Fresno nightcrawler was first popularized by CCTV footage shot in 2007 by a man in south Fresno, California, featured on the television network Univision.",

      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {

        "en": "It was first popularized by 2007 CCTV footage from south Fresno aired on Univision."
      }

    {
      "id": "fresno-nightcrawler-c06",
      "source_id": "fresno-nightcrawler-s1",
      "quote": "when Weird Fresno founder Michael Banti contacted regional tribes to confirm their authenticity, his contacts did not recognize the photographed sculptures as matching local indigenous monuments.",
      "locator": "History",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saat Michael Banti dari Weird Fresno menghubungi suku-suku setempat, mereka tidak mengenali patung yang difoto sebagai monumen pribumi setempat.",
        "en": "When Michael Banti of Weird Fresno checked with regional tribes, they did not recognize the photographed sculptures as local monuments."
      }
    },
    {

      "source_id": "fresno-nightcrawler-s1",
      "quote": "The Fresno nightcrawler has become an icon of Fresno, and has spawned merchandise and fans around the world.",

      "id": "gallinipper-mythology-c02",
      "source_id": "gallinipper-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "These creatures were said to be a species of giant mosquitoes so big that their bones could fence a 140-acre (57 ha) field.",
      "locator": "Introduction",

      "source_id": "gallinipper-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "A popular telling of the legends has the creature get its bill out of a tree trunk, with the animal being large enough to clear 140 acres of land during the struggle.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

      "source_id": "gallinipper-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Gallinipper tales were appropriated as a feature of minstrel shows, but have also appeared in American blues songs such as \"Mosquito Moan\" by Blind Lemon Jefferson",

      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {

    "en": "The Hudson River Monster (\"Kipsy\") is a serpentine water monster of New York folklore reported since 1886 [hudson-river-monster-c01].",

      "en": "The Hudson River Monster, popularly \"Kipsy\", is a serpentine water monster reported along the Hudson River and New York Harbor since 1886 [hudson-river-monster-c01].       "source_id": "hudson-river-monster-s1",
      "quote": "In New York folklore, the Hudson River Monster, popularly known as \"Kipsy\" or less commonly \"Keepsie\" or \"Libby\" (after the Statue of Liberty) is an aquatic serpentine monster reportedly spotted along the Hudson River and New York Harbor since 1886.",
      "locator": "Introduction",

      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat New York, Hudson River Monster (\"Kipsy\", juga \"Keepsie\" atau \"Libby\") adalah monster air mirip ular yang dilaporkan terlihat di Sungai Hudson dan Pelabuhan New York sejak 1886.",
        "en": "In New York folklore, the Hudson River Monster (\"Kipsy\", also \"Keepsie\" or \"Libby\") is a serpentine water monster reported along the Hudson River and New York Harbor since 1886."
      }
    },

      "id": "hudson-river-monster-c02",
      "source_id": "hudson-river-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The name \"Kipsy\" is believed to derive from Poughkeepsie, a city located along the river.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama \"Kipsy\" diyakini berasal dari Poughkeepsie, kota di tepi sungai itu.",
        "en": "The name \"Kipsy\" is believed to come from Poughkeepsie, a city on the river."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hudson-river-monster-c03",
      "source_id": "hudson-river-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The creature was first reported in a series of newspaper articles published in 1886, in which it is referred to simply as \"the sea serpent.\"",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini pertama kali dilaporkan dalam serangkaian artikel surat kabar tahun 1886 yang menyebutnya \"ular laut\".",
        "en": "It was first reported in 1886 newspaper articles that called it simply \"the sea serpent.\""
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hudson-river-monster-c04",
      "source_id": "hudson-river-monster-s1",
      "quote": "Though many sources allege that \"Kipsy\" was described in Native American legends or spotted by Henry Hudson's crew as the sailed up the Hudson River on the Halve Maen, none provide any documentation in support of these claims.",
      "locator": "Background",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "jenu-s1",
      "quote": "Jenu are most active during the winter because humans move less and are easier to hunt.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jenu paling aktif pada musim dingin karena manusia kurang bergerak dan lebih mudah diburu.",
        "en": "Jenu are most active in winter, when humans move less and are easier to hunt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "jenu-c05",
      "source_id": "jenu-s1",
      "quote": "Jenua are comparable to the Wendigo of Anishinaabe and Cree mythology (and, to a lesser extent, Sasquatch).",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jenu dapat dibandingkan dengan Wendigo dalam mitologi Anishinaabe dan Cree, dan sedikit dengan Sasquatch.",
        "en": "Jenu are comparable to the Wendigo of Anishinaabe and Cree mythology and, less so, to Sasquatch."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "La Bolefuego adalah arwah perempuan yang tampak seperti lentera terang pada malam di Los Llanos, dalam cerita rakyat Kolombia dan Venezuela [la-bolefuego-c01, la-bolefuego-c02, la-bolefuego-c03].",
    "en": "La Bolefuego is the spirit of a woman seen as a bright lantern on the nights of Los Llanos, in Colombian and Venezuelan folklore [la-bolefuego-c01, la-bolefuego-c02, la-bolefuego-c03].",

    {
      "id": "La Bolefuego atau La Candileja adalah tokoh legenda Kolombia dan Venezuela [la-bolefuego-c01]. Ia muncul sebagai lentera terang yang berputar pada malam gelap di Los Llanos [la-bolefuego-c02]. Ia konon arwah perempuan yang terbakar hidup-hidup bersama dua anaknya dan menyerang pelancong [la-bolefuego-c03]. Berbeda dengan makhluk jahat lain, ia justru tertarik oleh doa [la-bolefuego-c04].",
      "en": "La Bolefuego or La Candileja is a legendary character of Colombian and Venezuelan folklore [la-bolefuego-c01].   "short_description": {
    "id": "La Diablesse adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Karibia, perempuan yang menjual jiwanya kepada Iblis demi kecantikan abadi dan menyesatkan laki-laki ke hutan [la-diablesse-c01, la-diablesse-c03].",
    "en": "La Diablesse is a Caribbean folklore figure who sold her soul to the Devil for eternal beauty and lures men into the forest [la-diablesse-c01, la-diablesse-c03].",
    "claim_ids": [
      "la-diablesse-c01",
      "la-diablesse-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "La Diablesse adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Karibia: perempuan Afrika yang diperbudak dan menyerahkan jiwanya kepada Iblis demi kecantikan abadi [la-diablesse-c01]. Wajahnya yang mengerikan tersembunyi di balik topi lebar dan satu kakinya berujung kuku sapi [la-diablesse-c02].",
      "en": "La Diablesse is a Caribbean folklore figure: an enslaved African woman who gave her soul to the Devil for eternal beauty [la-diablesse-c01]. Her hideous face hides under a wide hat and one leg ends in a cow hoof [la-diablesse-c02].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "la-diablesse-c01",
        "la-diablesse-c02"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia menyihir korban laki-laki, membawanya ke hutan, lalu menghilang [la-diablesse-c03]; korban tersesat hingga jatuh ke jurang atau sungai [la-diablesse-c04]. Derek Walcott menyebutnya dalam drama Dream on Monkey Mountain [la-diablesse-c05].",
      "en": "She bewitches a male victim, leads him into the forest and vanishes [la-diablesse-c03]; he runs lost until he falls into a ravine or river [la-diablesse-c04]. Derek Walcott refers to her in the play Dream on Monkey Mountain [la-diablesse-c05].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "la-diablesse-c03",
        "la-diablesse-c04",
        "la-diablesse-c05"

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "la-diablesse-c01",
      "source_id": "la-diablesse-s1",
      "quote": "is a character in Caribbean folklore. According to folklore, she was an enslaved African woman who made a deal with Le Diable (the Devil) to give her soul to him, becoming a demi-human, in exchange for eternal beauty.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "La Diablesse adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Karibia; menurut kisahnya, ia perempuan Afrika yang diperbudak dan menyerahkan jiwanya kepada Iblis demi kecantikan abadi, lalu menjadi setengah manusia.",
        "en": "La Diablesse is a figure of Caribbean folklore; she was an enslaved African woman who gave her soul to the Devil for eternal beauty, becoming a demi-human."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-diablesse-c02",
      "source_id": "la-diablesse-s1",
      "quote": "However, her hideous face is hidden by a large brimmed hat, and her long dress hides the fact that one leg ends in a cow hoof.",
      "locator": "Depiction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wajahnya yang mengerikan tersembunyi di balik topi lebar, dan gaun panjangnya menutupi satu kaki yang berujung kuku sapi.",
        "en": "Her hideous face is hidden by a wide hat, and her long dress hides one leg ending in a cow hoof."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-diablesse-c03",
      "source_id": "la-diablesse-s1",
      "quote": "She can cast spells on her unsuspecting male victim, whom she leads into the forest. When in the forest, she disappears.",
      "locator": "Depiction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat menyihir korban laki-laki dan membawanya ke hutan, lalu menghilang.",
        "en": "She can bewitch an unsuspecting male victim, lead him into the forest and then vanish."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-diablesse-c04",
      "source_id": "la-diablesse-s1",
      "quote": "The victim (confused, lost, and scared) runs around the forest until he falls into a ravine or river, or gets eaten.",
      "locator": "Depiction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Korban yang bingung dan tersesat berlarian di hutan sampai jatuh ke jurang atau sungai, atau dimangsa.",
        "en": "The confused, lost victim runs through the forest until he falls into a ravine or river, or is eaten."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-diablesse-c05",
      "source_id": "la-diablesse-s1",
      "quote": "La Diablesse is referred by Derek Walcott in his play Dream on Monkey Mountain.",
      "locator": "In popular culture",
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Derek Walcott menyebut La Diablesse dalam dramanya Dream on Monkey Mountain.",

    "id": "Lagahoo adalah monster pengubah wujud dalam cerita rakyat Trinidad and Tobago yang berkeliaran pada malam hari tanpa kepala dengan peti mati di lehernya [lagahoo-c01, lagahoo-c02].",
    "en": "The Lagahoo is a shapeshifting monster of Trinidad and Tobago folklore that roams at night headless, with a coffin on its neck [lagahoo-c01, lagahoo-c02].",
    "claim_ids": [

  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lagahoo adalah monster pengubah wujud dalam cerita rakyat Trinidad and Tobago [lagahoo-c01]. Pada siang hari ia tampak seperti manusia, tetapi pada malam hari menjadi lelaki tanpa kepala dengan peti mati di lehernya [lagahoo-c02], dihiasi tiga lilin menyala dan rantai besi yang terseret [lagahoo-c03].",
      "en": "The Lagahoo is a shapeshifting monster of Trinidad and Tobago folklore [lagahoo-c01]. Human by day, at night it becomes a headless man with a coffin on its neck [lagahoo-c02], topped by three lit candles, dragging an iron chain [lagahoo-c03].",
      "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Ia dapat berubah menjadi kuda, babi, kambing, atau makhluk mirip centaur [lagahoo-c04] serta mengubah ukurannya dalam sekejap [lagahoo-c05]. Untuk membunuhnya, ia harus dipukuli dengan tongkat yang diurapi air dan minyak suci selama sembilan hari [lagahoo-c06].",
      "en": "It can become a horse, pig, goat or centaur-like creature [lagahoo-c04] and change its size in an instant [lagahoo-c05]. To kill it, one must beat it with a stick anointed with holy water and oil for nine days [lagahoo-c06].",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c01",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "In the folklore of Trinidad and Tobago the Lagahoo or Lugarhou is a mythical shapeshifting monster.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Trinidad and Tobago, Lagahoo atau Lugarhou adalah monster mitos pengubah wujud.",
        "en": "In the folklore of Trinidad and Tobago, the Lagahoo or Lugarhou is a mythical shapeshifting monster."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c02",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "It seems like a normal human by day, but this creature takes on the form of a man with no head, who roams the night with a wooden coffin on its neck.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada siang hari ia tampak seperti manusia biasa, tetapi pada malam hari menjadi lelaki tanpa kepala yang berkeliaran dengan peti mati kayu di lehernya.",
        "en": "By day it seems human, but at night it becomes a headless man roaming with a wooden coffin on its neck."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c03",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "On top of the coffin are three lighted candles and the long loose end of a heavy iron chain, noosed around its waist, trails behind him.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di atas peti itu ada tiga lilin menyala, dan rantai besi berat yang terikat di pinggangnya terseret di belakangnya.",
        "en": "Three lit candles sit on the coffin, and a heavy iron chain tied at its waist trails behind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c04",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "It can shapeshift into various animals, including horses, pigs or goats, and said to often take the form of a creature similar to a centaur",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat berubah menjadi berbagai hewan seperti kuda, babi, atau kambing, dan sering berwujud mirip centaur.",
        "en": "It can change into horses, pigs or goats and often takes a centaur-like form."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c05",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "The Lagahoo also possesses the ability to alter its size from tiny to gigantic in an instant.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lagahoo juga dapat mengubah ukurannya dari sangat kecil menjadi raksasa dalam sekejap.",
        "en": "The Lagahoo can change its size from tiny to gigantic in an instant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lagahoo-c06",
      "source_id": "lagahoo-s1",
      "quote": "To kill the Lagahoo one must beat the creature with a stick which has been anointed with holy water and holy oil for nine days.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk membunuhnya, Lagahoo harus dipukuli dengan tongkat yang telah diurapi air suci dan minyak suci selama sembilan hari.",
        "en": "To kill it, one must beat it with a stick anointed with holy water and holy oil for nine days."
      }
    }

      "quote": "It is said that it inhabits rivers and lakes, which never dry out while it lives there.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon menghuni sungai dan danau, yang tidak pernah kering selama ia tinggal di sana.",
        "en": "She inhabits rivers and lakes, which never dry while she lives there."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "madre-de-aguas-c06",
      "source_id": "madre-de-aguas-s1",
      "quote": "Madre de aguas never dies, and anyone who tries to kill or capture it dies.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Madre de aguas tidak pernah mati, dan siapa pun yang mencoba membunuh atau menangkapnya akan mati.",
        "en": "The Madre de aguas never dies, and anyone who tries to kill or capture her dies."
      }
    }

      "id": "Mulher da meia-noite adalah hantu dalam cerita rakyat dunia [midnight-beauty-c01] yang kisahnya paling beragam di Amerika Selatan [midnight-beauty-c02]. Di Brasil ia disebut juga Bela da Noite atau A Mulher de branco [midnight-beauty-c03]; di Venezuela disebut La Sayona [midnight-beauty-c04] dan di Meksiko La Llorona [midnight-beauty-c05].",
      "en": "The mulher da meia-noite is a ghost of world folklore [midnight-beauty-c01] whose tales are most varied in South America [midnight-beauty-c02]. In Brazil she is also Bela da Noite or A Mulher de branco [midnight-beauty-c03]; in Venezuela, La Sayona [midnight-beauty-c04]; in Mexico, La Llorona [midnight-beauty-c05].",

      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "A mulher da meia-noite é um fantasma do folclore mundial.",
      "locator": "Introdução",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mulher da meia-noite (\"perempuan tengah malam\") adalah hantu dalam cerita rakyat dunia.",
        "en": "The mulher da meia-noite (\"midnight woman\") is a ghost of world folklore."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c02",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "Sua história possui diversas variantes em várias partes do mundo, mas é na América do Sul que encontram relatos mais variados.",
      "locator": "Introdução",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisahnya punya banyak varian di berbagai belahan dunia, dan kisah paling beragam ditemukan di Amerika Selatan.",
        "en": "Her story has many variants worldwide, with the most varied accounts in South America."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c03",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "No Brasil, a Mulher da meia-noite também recebe os nomes de Bela (ou Dama) da Noite, A Mulher de branco",
      "locator": "Versões",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Brasil ia juga disebut Bela (atau Dama) da Noite dan A Mulher de branco (\"perempuan berbaju putih\").",
        "en": "In Brazil she is also called Bela (or Dama) da Noite and A Mulher de branco (\"the woman in white\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c04",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "Na Venezuela, é chamada de \"La Sayona\".",
      "locator": "Versões",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Venezuela ia disebut \"La Sayona\".",
        "en": "In Venezuela she is called \"La Sayona\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c05",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "No México recebeu o nome de \"La Llorona\"",
      "locator": "Versões",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Meksiko ia dinamai \"La Llorona\".",
        "en": "In Mexico she was named \"La Llorona\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c06",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "Uma bela jovem que aparece em estradas e pede carona para os homens.",
      "locator": "Brasil",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu versi Brasil: perempuan muda cantik yang muncul di jalan dan meminta tumpangan kepada laki-laki.",
        "en": "One Brazilian version: a beautiful young woman who appears on roads asking men for rides."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "midnight-beauty-c07",
      "source_id": "midnight-beauty-s1",
      "quote": "Se ele realmente trair, ela poderá matá-lo; caso contrário, ela apenas irá feri-lo.",
      "locator": "Brasil",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jika laki-laki itu benar-benar berselingkuh, ia dapat membunuhnya; jika tidak, ia hanya melukainya.",
        "en": "If the man truly betrays his partner she may kill him; otherwise she only wounds him."
      }
    }

        "en": "Otomitl is one of the six giant sons of Iztac-Mixcoatl and Tlaltecuhtli who populated the Earth after the Great Flood in the Fifth Sun of Aztec mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "otomitl-c02",
      "source_id": "otomitl-s1",
      "quote": "The sixth son who climbed the mountains near Mexico, and raised the populations of Xilotepec, Tollan, and Otompan.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia putra keenam yang mendaki pegunungan dekat Mexico dan membangun permukiman Xilotepec, Tollan, dan Otompan.",
        "en": "He was the sixth son, who climbed the mountains near Mexico and raised the settlements of Xilotepec, Tollan and Otompan."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Pincoy adalah roh laut laki-laki dalam mitologi Chilote, putra Millalobo, yang lagunya memulai tarian kesuburan Pincoya [pincoy-c01, pincoy-c03, pincoy-c05].",
    "en": "The Pincoy is a male sea spirit of Chilote mythology, son of Millalobo, whose song starts the Pincoya's fertility dance [pincoy-c01, pincoy-c03, pincoy-c05].",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pincoy-c01",
      "pincoy-c03",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pincoy adalah roh air laut laki-laki dalam mitologi Chilote [pincoy-c01]. Ia mirip manusia duyung bertubuh singa laut berwarna emas dan berwajah tampan [pincoy-c02]. Ia putra Millalobo dan Huenchula; saudarinya adalah Sirena chilota dan Pincoya, yang juga istrinya [pincoy-c03].",
      "en": "The Pincoy is a male sea spirit of Chilote mythology [pincoy-c01]. He resembles a merman with a golden sea-lion body and a handsome face [pincoy-c02]. He is the son of Millalobo and Huenchula; his sisters are the Sirena chilota and the Pincoya, also his wife [pincoy-c03].",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pincoy-c01",
        "pincoy-c02",
        "pincoy-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Bersama saudarinya ia membawa orang mati dari laut ke Caleuche [pincoy-c04], dan lagunya membuat Pincoya memulai tarian gaib demi kesuburan laut [pincoy-c05].",
      "en": "With his sisters he carries the dead from the sea to the Caleuche [pincoy-c04], and his song starts the Pincoya's magical dance for the sea's fertility [pincoy-c05].",

      "source_id": "qailertetang-s1",

    "en": "The muki is a dwarfish, goblin-like creature of Central Andean mythology that lives inside the mines [muki-mythology-c01, muki-mythology-c02, muki-mythology-c03].",
    "claim_ids": [

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Muki adalah makhluk mirip goblin dalam mitologi Andes Tengah [muki-mythology-c01] yang hidup di dalam tambang [muki-mythology-c02]. Tingginya tidak lebih dari 2 kaki [muki-mythology-c03], dan dalam beberapa tradisi ia bertanduk dua untuk memecah batu dan menunjuk urat mineral [muki-mythology-c04]. Ia bisa menyamar sebagai hewan atau lelaki berambut pirang untuk menipu penambang [muki-mythology-c05]; para tetua menyarankan melawannya dengan ikat pinggang [muki-mythology-c06].",
      "en": "The muki is a goblin-like creature of Central Andean mythology [muki-mythology-c01] that lives inside the mines [muki-mythology-c02]. He is no taller than 2 feet [muki-mythology-c03], and in some traditions has two horns to break rocks and point out veins [muki-mythology-c04]. He may disguise himself as an animal or a blond man to deceive miners [muki-mythology-c05]; elders advise fighting him with a belt [muki-mythology-c06].",

      "source_id": "muki-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "He is known to be a miner and his existence is constrained to underground spaces: The muki lives inside the mines.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dikenal sebagai penambang yang hanya hidup di ruang bawah tanah, yaitu di dalam tambang.",

      "source_id": "muki-mythology-s1",

    {
      "id": "muki-mythology-c05",
      "source_id": "muki-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Sometimes he shape-shifts into an animal or a blonde white man to appear to the miners and deceive them.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

        "en": "Sometimes he turns into an animal or a blond white man to deceive the miners."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muki-mythology-c06",
      "source_id": "muki-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Elders advise that, when dealing with the muki, one should use his/her belt to battle him without succumbing to fear.",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "mussie-s1",
      "quote": "The legend of Mussie likely began around 1916, though legend claims that Canadian pioneer Samuel de Champlain wrote about it in the early seventeenth century.",

    "id": "Seelkee adalah monster danau berkepala kuda dari rawa Chilliwack, British Columbia, yang konon dilihat orang Stó:lō selama ratusan tahun [seelkee-c01, seelkee-c02, seelkee-c03].",
    "en": "Seelkee is a horse-headed lake monster of the Chilliwack swamps, British Columbia, reportedly seen by the Stó:lō for centuries [seelkee-c01, seelkee-c02, seelkee-c03].",

    {
      "id": "Seelkee adalah monster danau yang konon hidup di rawa-rawa Chilliwack, British Columbia [seelkee-c01], dan telah dilihat orang Stó:lō selama ratusan tahun [seelkee-c02]. Ia paling sering digambarkan seperti ular laut berkepala kuda sepanjang 10 hingga 15 kaki [seelkee-c03], atau ular berkepala dua berwarna hitam berpola merah [seelkee-c04]. Motifnya menghiasi tiang rumah panjang Stó:lō [seelkee-c05].",
      "en": "Seelkee is a lake monster said to live in the swamps of Chilliwack, British Columbia [seelkee-c01], seen by the Stó:lō for centuries [seelkee-c02].       "id": "seelkee-c01",
      "source_id": "seelkee-s1",
      "quote": "In Canadian folklore, Seelkee (transcribed in English from Halqemeylem, the local indigenous language) is a lake monster reported to have lived in the swamps of what is now Chilliwack, in British Columbia, Canada.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Kanada, Seelkee (dari bahasa Halqemeylem) adalah monster danau yang konon hidup di rawa-rawa wilayah yang kini menjadi Chilliwack, British Columbia.",
        "en": "In Canadian folklore, Seelkee (from the Halqemeylem language) is a lake monster said to have lived in the swamps of present-day Chilliwack, British Columbia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "seelkee-c02",
      "source_id": "seelkee-s1",
      "quote": "Seelkee has been allegedly seen by the Stó:lō, First Nations, people for hundreds of years.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seelkee konon telah dilihat orang Stó:lō selama ratusan tahun.",
        "en": "Seelkee has reportedly been seen by the Stó:lō people for hundreds of years."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "seelkee-c03",
      "source_id": "seelkee-s1",
      "quote": "The most common description of Seelkee is a 10- to 15-foot-long (3.0 to 4.6 m) sea serpent-like beast with the head of a horse.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran paling umum adalah makhluk mirip ular laut sepanjang 10 hingga 15 kaki berkepala kuda.",
        "en": "Most commonly it is a 10- to 15-foot sea-serpent-like beast with a horse's head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "seelkee-c04",
      "source_id": "seelkee-s1",
      "quote": "Descriptions often portray the creature as snake-like with two heads. Furthermore, the coloration has been described as mostly black with red circular designs.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga sering digambarkan seperti ular berkepala dua, berwarna hitam dengan pola lingkaran merah.",
        "en": "It is often portrayed as a two-headed snake, mostly black with red circular designs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "seelkee-c05",
      "source_id": "seelkee-s1",
      "quote": "Some of the longhouses in the local Stó:lō villages were defined by large house posts with Seelkee designs accented with red paint.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Beberapa rumah panjang di desa Stó:lō memiliki tiang besar bermotif Seelkee dengan cat merah.",
        "en": "Some Stó:lō longhouses had large house posts with red-painted Seelkee designs."
      }
    }

    "id": "Si-Te-Cah adalah suku legendaris berambut merah dalam sejarah lisan Paiute Utara, yang dalam versi belakangan digambarkan sebagai raksasa [si-te-cah-c01, si-te-cah-c02].",
    "en": "The Si-Te-Cah were a legendary red-haired tribe of Northern Paiute oral history, described as giants in later versions [si-te-cah-c01, si-te-cah-c02].",

    {
      "id": "Menurut sejarah lisan Paiute Utara, Si-Te-Cah adalah suku legendaris yang diperangi dan diusir, dengan pertempuran terakhir di Lovelock Cave [si-te-cah-c01]. Mereka konon berambut merah, kadang kanibal, dan dalam versi belakangan raksasa [si-te-cah-c02]. Namanya berarti \"pemakan tule\" [si-te-cah-c03], dan mereka konon tinggal di rakit tule di Danau Humboldt [si-te-cah-c04].",
      "en": "In Northern Paiute oral history, the Si-Te-Cah were a legendary tribe fought and driven out, with a final battle at Lovelock Cave [si-te-cah-c01]. They were said to be red-haired, sometimes cannibals, and in later versions giants [si-te-cah-c02]. Their name means \"tule-eaters\" [si-te-cah-c03], and they reportedly lived on tule rafts on Lake Humboldt [si-te-cah-c04].",

      "source_id": "si-te-cah-s1",
      "quote": "In order to escape harassment from the Paiutes, the Si-Te-Cahs were said to have lived on rafts made of tule on Lake Humboldt.",

      "source_id": "si-te-cah-s1",

      "quote": "La Tulevieja (also spelled Tulivieja) is a legendary figure from Costa Rican and Panamanian folklore. She is a ghost who wears a distinctive hat called a tule.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "La Tulevieja (juga Tulivieja) adalah tokoh legenda cerita rakyat Kosta Rika dan Panama, hantu bertopi khas yang disebut tule.",
        "en": "La Tulevieja (also Tulivieja) is a legendary figure of Costa Rican and Panamanian folklore, a ghost wearing a distinctive hat called a tule."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tulevieja-c02",
      "source_id": "tulevieja-s1",
      "quote": "She is also commonly described as a hybrid of woman and bird (similar to a harpy)",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga sering digambarkan sebagai campuran perempuan dan burung, mirip harpy.",
        "en": "She is also often described as a woman-bird hybrid, like a harpy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tulevieja-c03",
      "source_id": "tulevieja-s1",
      "quote": "her most characteristic features are her legs and claws of an eagle or hawk instead of human legs, which leave reversed footprints so that she cannot be followed.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri utamanya adalah kaki dan cakar elang yang meninggalkan jejak terbalik sehingga ia tidak bisa diikuti.",
        "en": "Her hallmark is eagle or hawk legs and claws that leave reversed footprints so she cannot be followed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tulevieja-c04",
      "source_id": "tulevieja-s1",
      "quote": "She is said to feed on charcoal and ashes, which is why it is common to find her tracks around recently extinguished campfires.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon memakan arang dan abu, sehingga jejaknya sering ditemukan di sekitar api unggun yang baru padam.",
        "en": "She reportedly eats charcoal and ashes, so her tracks appear near recently doused campfires."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tulevieja-c05",
      "source_id": "tulevieja-s1",
      "quote": "The only way to save oneself once encountering her is to recite the prayer \"Alabado sea el Santísimo\" (\"Praised be the Most Holy Sacrament\"), which causes her to take flight and disappear toward the sun.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Satu-satunya cara menyelamatkan diri adalah mendaraskan doa \"Alabado sea el Santísimo\", yang membuatnya terbang dan menghilang ke arah matahari.",
        "en": "The only escape is to recite \"Alabado sea el Santísimo\", which makes her fly off toward the sun."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "images",

}

    "id": "Turtle Lake Monster adalah makhluk dalam cerita rakyat Kanada yang konon menghuni Turtle Lake, Saskatchewan [turtle-lake-monster-c01].",
    "en": "The Turtle Lake Monster is a Canadian folklore creature said to inhabit Turtle Lake, Saskatchewan [turtle-lake-monster-c01].",
    "claim_ids": [
      "turtle-lake-monster-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Turtle Lake Monster konon menghuni Turtle Lake di Saskatchewan [turtle-lake-monster-c01]. Ia digambarkan sepanjang 3 hingga 9 meter dengan kepala mirip anjing, kuda laut, atau babi [turtle-lake-monster-c02]. Orang Cree punya legenda tentang orang yang hilang di wilayahnya [turtle-lake-monster-c03]. Penampakannya mungkin ikan sturgeon besar [turtle-lake-monster-c04].",
      "en": "The Turtle Lake Monster reportedly inhabits Turtle Lake in Saskatchewan [turtle-lake-monster-c01]. Sightings may be of a large sturgeon [turtle-lake-monster-c04].",
      "claim_ids": [

      "id": "turtle-lake-monster-s1",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "turtle-lake-monster-c01",
      "source_id": "turtle-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "In Canadian folklore, the Turtle Lake Monster is an entity purportedly inhabiting Turtle Lake, in West Central Saskatchewan, Canada.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Kanada, Turtle Lake Monster adalah makhluk yang konon menghuni Turtle Lake di Saskatchewan barat-tengah.",
        "en": "In Canadian folklore, the Turtle Lake Monster reportedly inhabits Turtle Lake in West Central Saskatchewan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "turtle-lake-monster-c02",
      "source_id": "turtle-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "The monster is usually described as a creature 3–9 m (9.8–29.5 ft) long, scaly or smooth, with no dorsal fin, and a head resembling either a dog, a seahorse, or a pig.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster ini biasanya digambarkan sepanjang 3 hingga 9 meter, tanpa sirip punggung, dengan kepala mirip anjing, kuda laut, atau babi.",
        "en": "It is usually described as 3 to 9 m long, with no dorsal fin and a head like a dog, seahorse or pig."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "turtle-lake-monster-c03",
      "source_id": "turtle-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "Reports date back to pre-settlement days when the local Cree had a legend about people who ventured into the Turtle Lake Monster's territory and vanished without a trace.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporannya sudah ada sebelum masa pemukiman, ketika orang Cree punya legenda tentang orang yang masuk wilayahnya dan hilang tanpa jejak.",
        "en": "Reports go back to pre-settlement days, when the Cree told of people who entered its territory and vanished."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "turtle-lake-monster-c04",
      "source_id": "turtle-lake-monster-s1",
      "quote": "There is speculation that the monster sightings may be attributed to sightings of an unusually large lake sturgeon, or a relict population of prehistoric plesiosaurs.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada dugaan penampakannya adalah ikan sturgeon danau yang sangat besar atau sisa populasi plesiosaurus.",
        "en": "Sightings may be of an unusually large lake sturgeon, or relict plesiosaurs."
      }
    }
  ],

      "source_id": "unhcegila-s1",
      "quote": "is a serpentoid creature which was responsible for many unexplained disappearances and deaths. Her male counterpart is known as Unk Tehi.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Lakota, Unhcegila adalah makhluk mirip ular yang dianggap penyebab banyak kematian dan kehilangan misterius; pasangan jantannya bernama Unk Tehi.",
        "en": "In Lakota mythology, Unhcegila is a serpent-like creature blamed for many unexplained disappearances and deaths; her male counterpart is Unk Tehi."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unhcegila-c02",
      "source_id": "unhcegila-s1",
      "quote": "As time went on further, her form was exposed as being massive, with a long scaly body whose natural armor was almost impenetrable.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Belakangan wujudnya terungkap sangat besar, bertubuh panjang bersisik dengan pelindung alami yang hampir tak tertembus.",

      }
    },

      "id": "unhcegila-c03",
      "source_id": "unhcegila-s1",
      "quote": "Whoever looked upon her will become blind or go insane.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Siapa pun yang memandangnya akan buta atau gila.",
        "en": "Whoever looks upon her goes blind or insane."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "unhcegila-c04",
      "source_id": "unhcegila-s1",
      "quote": "Her weakness is the seventh spot on her torso, behind which lies her heart. To kill her, one has to shoot a medicine arrow at this flaw in her natural armor.",
      "locator": "Description",
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kelemahannya adalah titik ketujuh di badannya, tempat jantungnya berada; untuk membunuhnya, panah obat harus ditembakkan ke titik itu.",
        "en": "Her weakness is the seventh spot on her torso, over her heart; to kill her, one must shoot a medicine arrow there."
      }
    }
  ],

      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahöla adalah salah satu katsinam kepala yang penting bagi First dan Second Mesa karena ia membuka upacara pertengahan musim dingin Powamu, festival menanam kacang.",
        "en": "Ahöla is an important chief katsinam for First and Second Mesas, opening the mid-winter Powamu ceremony, the bean planting festival."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ahola-c03",
      "source_id": "ahola-s1",
      "quote": "At the end of the ceremony, Ahöla descends to a shrine, bows four times to the sun, and asks for health, happiness, long life, and good crops.",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di akhir upacara, Ahöla turun ke sebuah tempat suci, membungkuk empat kali kepada matahari, dan memohon kesehatan, kebahagiaan, umur panjang, dan panen yang baik.",
        "en": "At the end, Ahöla goes to a shrine, bows four times to the sun and asks for health, happiness, long life and good crops."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ahola-c04",
      "source_id": "ahola-s1",
      "quote": "Ahöla is also the friend of Eototo",
      "locator": "Introduction",
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahöla juga sahabat Eototo.",
        "en": "Ahöla is also the friend of Eototo."
      }
    }

      "quote": "Diz a lenda que o Negro-d'Água ou Nego-d'Água habita diversos rios, tais como o Rio Tocantins, o Rio Grande, Rio Grande, oeste da Bahia e Rio São Francisco",

      "statement": {

      "source_id": "akva-nigrulo-s1",

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {

      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
